NIU THIRD-PARTY NOTICES
=======================

This self-contained aggregate lists the licenses of every third-party Rust
crate compiled into niu-natives (the Niu desktop-automation native extension).
It does not replace package-level license files; first-party licensing grants
no rights in third-party code identified below.

Provenance: generated from `cargo vendor` of niu-natives/Cargo.lock
(2026-09-09). Each crate's vendored source tree was inspected; license texts
below are verbatim copies of the LICENSE/COPYING files shipped in each crate's
published source. Identical license texts are printed once and all crates
using that text are listed under it.

Coverage: 303 packages in niu-natives/Cargo.lock (including the
first-party niu-natives itself, which is licensed under MIT — see
niu-natives/LICENSE). Reconciliation: every lock entry was matched to a
vendored source directory; zero unmatched entries. Re-reconciled
2026-09-13 after the Windows UIA dependency (uiautomation) was added:
6 packages appended (uiautomation, uiautomation_derive, chrono,
iana-time-zone, iana-time-zone-haiku, android_system_properties);
their license texts are verbatim copies from the local cargo registry
source cache (~/.cargo/registry/src).

===============================================================================
FIRST-PARTY PROVENANCE — oh-my-pi (pi-natives desktop module)

crate of oh-my-pi (https://github.com/badlogic/oh-my-pi), which is

===============================================================================
PART 2 — CRATES WITHOUT A LICENSE FILE IN THE PUBLISHED SOURCE
===============================================================================

The following crates ship no LICENSE/COPYING file in their published source
tree (verified via cargo vendor). Their license was determined from package
metadata and/or the upstream repository (checked 2026-09-09); the linked
source carries the full license text and must be retained when
redistributing their code.

- block2 0.6.2 — license: MIT
  basis: madsmtm/objc2 workspace — per repo LICENSE.md, crates objc2/block2/objc2-foundation/objc2-encode are MIT-licensed
  source: https://github.com/madsmtm/objc2/blob/main/LICENSE-MIT.txt
- cookie-factory 0.3.3 — license: MIT
  basis: declared in package metadata; LICENSE file in upstream repository
  source: https://github.com/rust-bakery/cookie-factory
- dispatch2 0.3.1 — license: Zlib OR Apache-2.0 OR MIT
  basis: madsmtm/objc2 workspace — per repo LICENSE.md, all crates except objc2/block2/objc2-foundation/objc2-encode are trio-licensed (Zlib/Apache-2.0/MIT)
  source: https://github.com/madsmtm/objc2/blob/main/LICENSE.md
